+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* __restrict__ points,
+                                    double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                    int point_count,
+                                    size_t pair_count) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (index >= pair_count) return;
+    const int member = static_cast<int>(index / point_count);
+    const int candidate = static_cast<int>(index - static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count);
+    const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+    const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+    distances[index] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ bool betterCandidate(double lhs_distance, int lhs_index,
+                                                double rhs_distance, int rhs_index) {
+    return lhs_distance < rhs_distance ||
+           (lhs_distance == rhs_distance && lhs_index < rhs_index);
+}
+
+// Each block independently constructs one seed's greedy candidate cluster. Threads
+// cooperate over candidates; blocks expose parallelism over all currently live seeds.
+// max_distance is updated only for the newly inserted member, which is algebraically
+// identical to rescanning all members on every iteration.
+__global__ void constructCandidateClusters(const Point* __restrict__ points,
+                                           const double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                           const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                           const int* __restrict__ seeds,
+                                           int seed_offset,
+                                           int point_count,
+                                           double threshold,
+                                           double* __restrict__ workspace,
+                                           int* __restrict__ cardinalities,
+                                           int* __restrict__ stored_members) {
+    __shared__ double reduction_distance[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int reduction_index[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int current_member;
+    __shared__ int member_count;
+
+    const int tid = threadIdx.x;
+    const int seed_position = seed_offset + static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x);
+    const int seed = seeds[seed_position];
+    double* const max_distance = workspace + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * point_count;
+
+    for (int candidate = tid; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+        max_distance[candidate] = (clustered[candidate] || candidate == seed) ? -1.0 : 0.0;
     }
-    
-    // Copy members if requested
-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;
+    if (tid == 0) {
+        current_member = seed;
+        member_count = 1;
+        if (stored_members) stored_members[0] = seed;
     }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (current_member >= 0) {
+        const int member = current_member;
+        double local_distance = DBL_MAX;
+        int local_index = INT_MAX;
+
+        for (int candidate = tid; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            double candidate_distance = max_distance[candidate];
+            if (candidate_distance < 0.0) continue;
+
+            double new_distance;
+            if (distances) {
+                new_distance = distances[static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count + candidate];
+            } else {
+                const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+                const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+                new_distance = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+            }
+            candidate_distance = fmax(candidate_distance, new_distance);
+            max_distance[candidate] = candidate_distance;
+            if (candidate_distance < threshold &&
+                betterCandidate(candidate_distance, candidate, local_distance, local_index)) {
+                local_distance = candidate_distance;
+                local_index = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+
+        reduction_distance[tid] = local_distance;
+        reduction_index[tid] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (tid < stride &&
+                betterCandidate(reduction_distance[tid + stride], reduction_index[tid + stride],
+                                reduction_distance[tid], reduction_index[tid])) {
+                reduction_distance[tid] = reduction_distance[tid + stride];
+                reduction_index[tid] = reduction_index[tid + stride];
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+
+        if (tid == 0) {
+            const int selected = reduction_index[0] == INT_MAX ? -1 : reduction_index[0];
+            if (selected >= 0) {
+                max_distance[selected] = -1.0;
+                if (stored_members) stored_members[member_count] = selected;
+                ++member_count;
+            }
+            current_member = selected;
+            if (selected < 0) cardinalities[seed_position] = member_count;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
 }

+            if (tid < stride &&
+                betterCandidate(reduction_distance[tid + stride], reduction_index[tid + stride],
+                                reduction_distance[tid], reduction_index[tid])) {
+                reduction_distance[tid] = reduction_distance[tid + stride];
+                reduction_index[tid] = reduction_index[tid + stride];
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+
+        if (tid == 0) {
+            const int selected = reduction_index[0] == INT_MAX ? -1 : reduction_index[0];
+            if (selected >= 0) {
+                max_distance[selected] = -1.0;
+                if (stored_members) stored_members[member_count] = selected;
+                ++member_count;
+            }
+            current_member = selected;
+            if (selected < 0) cardinalities[seed_position] = member_count;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
 }

+#define CUDA_CHECK(expr) checkCuda((expr), #expr)
+
+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* __restrict__ points,
+                                    double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                    int point_count,
+                                    size_t pair_count) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (index >= pair_count) return;
+    const int member = static_cast<int>(index / point_count);
+    const int candidate = static_cast<int>(index - static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count);
+    const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+    const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+    distances[index] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ bool betterCandidate(double lhs_distance, int lhs_index,
+                                                double rhs_distance, int rhs_index) {
+    return lhs_distance < rhs_distance ||
+           (lhs_distance == rhs_distance && lhs_index < rhs_index);
+}
+
+// Each block independently constructs one seed's greedy candidate cluster. Threads
+// cooperate over candidates; blocks expose parallelism over all currently live seeds.
+// max_distance is updated only for the newly inserted member, which is algebraically
+// identical to rescanning all members on every iteration.
+__global__ void constructCandidateClusters(const Point* __restrict__ points,
+                                           const double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                           const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                           const int* __restrict__ seeds,
+                                           int seed_offset,
+                                           int point_count,
+                                           double threshold,
+                                           double* __restrict__ workspace,
+                                           int* __restrict__ cardinalities,
+                                           int* __restrict__ stored_members) {
+    __shared__ double reduction_distance[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int reduction_index[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int current_member;
+    __shared__ int member_count;
+
+    const int tid = threadIdx.x;
+    const int seed_position = seed_offset + static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x);
+    const int seed = seeds[seed_position];
+    double* const max_distance = workspace + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * point_count;
+
+    for (int candidate = tid; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+        max_distance[candidate] = (clustered[candidate] || candidate == seed) ? -1.0 : 0.0;
     }
-    
-    // Copy members if requested
-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;
+    if (tid == 0) {
+        current_member = seed;
+        member_count = 1;
+        if (stored_members) stored_members[0] = seed;
     }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (current_member >= 0) {
+        const int member = current_member;
+        double local_distance = DBL_MAX;
+        int local_index = INT_MAX;
+
+        for (int candidate = tid; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            double candidate_distance = max_distance[candidate];
+            if (candidate_distance < 0.0) continue;
+
+            double new_distance;
+            if (distances) {
+                new_distance = distances[static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count + candidate];
+            } else {
+                const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+                const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+                new_distance = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+            }
+            candidate_distance = fmax(candidate_distance, new_distance);
+            max_distance[candidate] = candidate_distance;
+            if (candidate_distance < threshold &&
+                betterCandidate(candidate_distance, candidate, local_distance, local_index)) {
+                local_distance = candidate_distance;
+                local_index = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+
+        reduction_distance[tid] = local_distance;
+        reduction_index[tid] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (tid < stride &&
+                betterCandidate(reduction_distance[tid + stride], reduction_index[tid + stride],
+                                reduction_distance[tid], reduction_index[tid])) {
+                reduction_distance[tid] = reduction_distance[tid + stride];
+                reduction_index[tid] = reduction_index[tid + stride];
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+
+        if (tid == 0) {
+            const int selected = reduction_index[0] == INT_MAX ? -1 : reduction_index[0];
+            if (selected >= 0) {
+                max_distance[selected] = -1.0;
+                if (stored_members) stored_members[member_count] = selected;
+                ++member_count;
+            }
+            current_member = selected;
+            if (selected < 0) cardinalities[seed_position] = member_count;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
 }

+#define CUDA_CHECK(expr) checkCuda((expr), #expr)

+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* __restrict__ points,
+                                    double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                    int point_count,
+                                    size_t pair_count) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (index >= pair_count) return;
+    const int member = static_cast<int>(index / point_count);
+    const int candidate = static_cast<int>(index - static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count);
+    const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+    const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+    distances[index] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ bool betterCandidate(double lhs_distance, int lhs_index,
+                                                double rhs_distance, int rhs_index) {
+    return lhs_distance < rhs_distance ||
+           (lhs_distance == rhs_distance && lhs_index < rhs_index);
+}
+
+// Each block independently constructs one seed's greedy candidate cluster. Threads
+// cooperate over candidates; blocks expose parallelism over all currently live seeds.
+// max_distance is updated only for the newly inserted member, which is algebraically
+// identical to rescanning all members on every iteration.
+__global__ void constructCandidateClusters(const Point* __restrict__ points,
+                                           const double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                           const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                           const int* __restrict__ seeds,
+                                           int seed_offset,
+                                           int point_count,
+                                           double threshold,
+                                           double* __restrict__ workspace,
+                                           int* __restrict__ cardinalities,
+                                           int* __restrict__ stored_members) {
+    __shared__ double reduction_distance[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int reduction_index[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int current_member;
+    __shared__ int member_count;
+
+    const int tid = threadIdx.x;
+    const int seed_position = seed_offset + static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x);
+    const int seed = seeds[seed_position];
+    double* const max_distance = workspace + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * point_count;
+
+    for (int candidate = tid; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+        max_distance[candidate] = (clustered[candidate] || candidate == seed) ? -1.0 : 0.0;
     }
-    
-    // Copy members if requested
-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;
+    if (tid == 0) {
+        current_member = seed;
+        member_count = 1;
+        if (stored_members) stored_members[0] = seed;
     }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (current_member >= 0) {
+        const int member = current_member;
+        double local_distance = DBL_MAX;
+        int local_index = INT_MAX;
+
+        for (int candidate = tid; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            double candidate_distance = max_distance[candidate];
+            if (candidate_distance < 0.0) continue;
+
+            double new_distance;
+            if (distances) {
+                new_distance = distances[static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count + candidate];
+            } else {
+                const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+                const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+                new_distance = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+            }
+            candidate_distance = fmax(candidate_distance, new_distance);
+            max_distance[candidate] = candidate_distance;
+            if (candidate_distance < threshold &&
+                betterCandidate(candidate_distance, candidate, local_distance, local_index)) {
+                local_distance = candidate_distance;
+                local_index = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+
+        reduction_distance[tid] = local_distance;
+        reduction_index[tid] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (tid < stride &&
+                betterCandidate(reduction_distance[tid + stride], reduction_index[tid + stride],
+                                reduction_distance[tid], reduction_index[tid])) {
+                reduction_distance[tid] = reduction_distance[tid + stride];
+                reduction_index[tid] = reduction_index[tid + stride];
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+
+        if (tid == 0) {
+            const int selected = reduction_index[0] == INT_MAX ? -1 : reduction_index[0];
+            if (selected >= 0) {
+                max_distance[selected] = -1.0;
+                if (stored_members) stored_members[member_count] = selected;
+                ++member_count;
+            }
+            current_member = selected;
+            if (selected < 0) cardinalities[seed_position] = member_count;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
 }

+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* __restrict__ points,
+                                    double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                    int point_count,
+                                    size_t pair_count) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (index >= pair_count) return;
+    const int member = static_cast<int>(index / point_count);
+    const int candidate = static_cast<int>(index - static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count);
+    const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+    const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+    distances[index] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ bool betterCandidate(double lhs_distance, int lhs_index,
+                                                double rhs_distance, int rhs_index) {
+    return lhs_distance < rhs_distance ||
+           (lhs_distance == rhs_distance && lhs_index < rhs_index);
+}
+
+// Each block independently constructs one seed's greedy candidate cluster. Threads
+// cooperate over candidates; blocks expose parallelism over all currently live seeds.
+// max_distance is updated only for the newly inserted member, which is algebraically
+// identical to rescanning all members on every iteration.
+__global__ void constructCandidateClusters(const Point* __restrict__ points,
+                                           const double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                           const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                           const int* __restrict__ seeds,
+                                           int seed_offset,
+                                           int point_count,
+                                           double threshold,
+                                           double* __restrict__ workspace,
+                                           int* __restrict__ cardinalities,
+                                           int* __restrict__ stored_members) {
+    __shared__ double reduction_distance[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int reduction_index[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int current_member;
+    __shared__ int member_count;
+
+    const int tid = threadIdx.x;
+    const int seed_position = seed_offset + static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x);
+    const int seed = seeds[seed_position];
+    double* const max_distance = workspace + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * point_count;
+
+    for (int candidate = tid; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+        max_distance[candidate] = (clustered[candidate] || candidate == seed) ? -1.0 : 0.0;
     }
-    
-    // Copy members if requested
-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;

+        current_member = seed;
+        member_count = 1;
+        if (stored_members) stored_members[0] = seed;
     }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (current_member >= 0) {
+        const int member = current_member;
+        double local_distance = DBL_MAX;
+        int local_index = INT_MAX;
+
+        for (int candidate = tid; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            double candidate_distance = max_distance[candidate];
+            if (candidate_distance < 0.0) continue;
+
+            double new_distance;
+            if (distances) {
+                new_distance = distances[static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count + candidate];
+            } else {
+                const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+                const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+                new_distance = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+            }
+            candidate_distance = fmax(candidate_distance, new_distance);
+            max_distance[candidate] = candidate_distance;
+            if (candidate_distance < threshold &&
+                betterCandidate(candidate_distance, candidate, local_distance, local_index)) {
+                local_distance = candidate_distance;
+                local_index = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+
+        reduction_distance[tid] = local_distance;
+        reduction_index[tid] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (tid < stride &&
+                betterCandidate(reduction_distance[tid + stride], reduction_index[tid + stride],
+                                reduction_distance[tid], reduction_index[tid])) {
+                reduction_distance[tid] = reduction_distance[tid + stride];
+                reduction_index[tid] = reduction_index[tid + stride];
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+
+        if (tid == 0) {
+            const int selected = reduction_index[0] == INT_MAX ? -1 : reduction_index[0];
+            if (selected >= 0) {
+                max_distance[selected] = -1.0;
+                if (stored_members) stored_members[member_count] = selected;
+                ++member_count;
+            }
+            current_member = selected;
+            if (selected < 0) cardinalities[seed_position] = member_count;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
 }

import ctypes, math
libc=ctypes.CDLL(None)
libc.rand_r.argtypes=[ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_uint)]
seed=ctypes.c_uint(42)
def frand(): return libc.rand_r(ctypes.byref(seed))/2147483647.0
N=100; pts=[None]*N; count=0
while count<N:
    cx=frand()*20; cy=frand()*20; R=frand()*10
    group=int(frand()*(N/30.0)); group=min(group,N-count)
    while group>0:
        sign=-1.0 if frand()<.5 else 1.0
        r=frand()*R; dx=(2*frand()-1)*r
        dy=math.sqrt(r*r-dx*dx)*sign; x=cx+dx; y=cy+dy
        if x<0 or x>20 or y<0 or y>20: continue
        pts[count]=(x,y); count+=1; group-=1
clustered=[False]*N; live=list(range(N)); out=[]
while live:
    best=None
    for seed_i in live:
        inside=[False]*N; inside[seed_i]=True; members=[seed_i]
        while len(members)<N:
            closest=-1; mind=float('inf')
            for cand in range(N):
                if clustered[cand] or inside[cand]: continue
                md=0.0
                for member in members:
                    dx=pts[cand][0]-pts[member][0]; dy=pts[cand][1]-pts[member][1]
                    md=max(md,math.sqrt(dx*dx+dy*dy))
                if md<2 and md<mind: mind=md; closest=cand
            if closest<0: break
            inside[closest]=True; members.append(closest)
        if best is None or len(members)>len(best): best=members
    out.append(best)
    for x in best: clustered[x]=True
    live=[x for x in live if not clustered[x]]
print('clusters',len(out),'max',max(map(len,out)))
for i,c in enumerate(out[:10]): print(i,len(c),c[0])
membership=[-1]*N

print('sum',sum(membership),'samples',[membership[x] for x in [0,25,50,75,99]])

+__device__ __forceinline__ bool betterCandidate(double lhs_distance, int lhs_index,
+                                                double rhs_distance, int rhs_index) {
+    return lhs_distance < rhs_distance ||
+           (lhs_distance == rhs_distance && lhs_index < rhs_index);
+}
+
+// Each block independently constructs one seed's greedy candidate cluster. Threads
+// cooperate over candidates; blocks expose parallelism over all currently live seeds.
+// max_distance is updated only for the newly inserted member, which is algebraically
+// identical to rescanning all members on every iteration.
+__global__ void constructCandidateClusters(const Point* __restrict__ points,
+                                           const double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                           const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                           const int* __restrict__ seeds,
+                                           int seed_offset,
+                                           int point_count,
+                                           double threshold,
+                                           double* __restrict__ workspace,
+                                           int* __restrict__ cardinalities,
+                                           int* __restrict__ stored_members) {
+    __shared__ double reduction_distance[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int reduction_index[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int current_member;
+    __shared__ int member_count;
+
+    const int tid = threadIdx.x;
+    const int seed_position = seed_offset + static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x);
+    const int seed = seeds[seed_position];
+    double* const max_distance = workspace + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * point_count;
+
+    for (int candidate = tid; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+        max_distance[candidate] = (clustered[candidate] || candidate == seed) ? -1.0 : 0.0;
     }
-    
-    // Copy members if requested
-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;

+        member_count = 1;
+        if (stored_members) stored_members[0] = seed;
     }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (current_member >= 0) {
+        const int member = current_member;
+        double local_distance = DBL_MAX;
+        int local_index = INT_MAX;
+
+        for (int candidate = tid; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            double candidate_distance = max_distance[candidate];
+            if (candidate_distance < 0.0) continue;
+
+            double new_distance;
+            if (distances) {
+                new_distance = distances[static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count + candidate];
+            } else {
+                const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+                const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+                new_distance = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+            }
+            candidate_distance = fmax(candidate_distance, new_distance);
+            max_distance[candidate] = candidate_distance;
+            if (candidate_distance < threshold &&
+                betterCandidate(candidate_distance, candidate, local_distance, local_index)) {
+                local_distance = candidate_distance;
+                local_index = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+
+        reduction_distance[tid] = local_distance;
+        reduction_index[tid] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (tid < stride &&
+                betterCandidate(reduction_distance[tid + stride], reduction_index[tid + stride],
+                                reduction_distance[tid], reduction_index[tid])) {
+                reduction_distance[tid] = reduction_distance[tid + stride];
+                reduction_index[tid] = reduction_index[tid + stride];
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+
+        if (tid == 0) {
+            const int selected = reduction_index[0] == INT_MAX ? -1 : reduction_index[0];
+            if (selected >= 0) {
+                max_distance[selected] = -1.0;
+                if (stored_members) stored_members[member_count] = selected;
+                ++member_count;
+            }
+            current_member = selected;
+            if (selected < 0) cardinalities[seed_position] = member_count;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
 }

+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* __restrict__ points,
+                                    double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                    int point_count,
+                                    size_t pair_count) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (index >= pair_count) return;
+    const int member = static_cast<int>(index / point_count);
+    const int candidate = static_cast<int>(index - static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count);
+    const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+    const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+    distances[index] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+}

+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* __restrict__ points,
+                                    double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                    int point_count,
+                                    size_t pair_count) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (index >= pair_count) return;
+    const int member = static_cast<int>(index / point_count);
+    const int candidate = static_cast<int>(index - static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count);
+    const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+    const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+    distances[index] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ bool betterCandidate(double lhs_distance, int lhs_index,
+                                                double rhs_distance, int rhs_index) {
+    return lhs_distance < rhs_distance ||
+           (lhs_distance == rhs_distance && lhs_index < rhs_index);
+}
+
+// Each block independently constructs one seed's greedy candidate cluster. Threads
+// cooperate over candidates; blocks expose parallelism over all currently live seeds.
+// max_distance is updated only for the newly inserted member, which is algebraically
+// identical to rescanning all members on every iteration.
+__global__ void constructCandidateClusters(const Point* __restrict__ points,
+                                           const double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                           const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                           const int* __restrict__ seeds,
+                                           int seed_offset,
+                                           int point_count,
+                                           double threshold,
+                                           double* __restrict__ workspace,
+                                           int* __restrict__ cardinalities,
+                                           int* __restrict__ stored_members) {
+    __shared__ double reduction_distance[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int reduction_index[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int current_member;
+    __shared__ int member_count;
+
+    const int tid = threadIdx.x;
+    const int seed_position = seed_offset + static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x);
+    const int seed = seeds[seed_position];
+    double* const max_distance = workspace + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * point_count;
+
+    for (int candidate = tid; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+        max_distance[candidate] = (clustered[candidate] || candidate == seed) ? -1.0 : 0.0;
     }
-    
-    // Copy members if requested
-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;

     }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (current_member >= 0) {
+        const int member = current_member;
+        double local_distance = DBL_MAX;
+        int local_index = INT_MAX;
+
+        for (int candidate = tid; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            double candidate_distance = max_distance[candidate];
+            if (candidate_distance < 0.0) continue;
+
+            double new_distance;
+            if (distances) {
+                new_distance = distances[static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count + candidate];
+            } else {
+                const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+                const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+                new_distance = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+            }
+            candidate_distance = fmax(candidate_distance, new_distance);
+            max_distance[candidate] = candidate_distance;
+            if (candidate_distance < threshold &&
+                betterCandidate(candidate_distance, candidate, local_distance, local_index)) {
+                local_distance = candidate_distance;
+                local_index = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+
+        reduction_distance[tid] = local_distance;
+        reduction_index[tid] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (tid < stride &&
+                betterCandidate(reduction_distance[tid + stride], reduction_index[tid + stride],
+                                reduction_distance[tid], reduction_index[tid])) {
+                reduction_distance[tid] = reduction_distance[tid + stride];
+                reduction_index[tid] = reduction_index[tid + stride];
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+
+        if (tid == 0) {
+            const int selected = reduction_index[0] == INT_MAX ? -1 : reduction_index[0];
+            if (selected >= 0) {
+                max_distance[selected] = -1.0;
+                if (stored_members) stored_members[member_count] = selected;
+                ++member_count;
+            }
+            current_member = selected;
+            if (selected < 0) cardinalities[seed_position] = member_count;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
 }

+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* __restrict__ points,
+                                    double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                    int point_count,
+                                    size_t pair_count) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (index >= pair_count) return;
+    const int member = static_cast<int>(index / point_count);
+    const int candidate = static_cast<int>(index - static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count);
+    const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+    const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+    distances[index] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ bool betterCandidate(double lhs_distance, int lhs_index,
+                                                double rhs_distance, int rhs_index) {
+    return lhs_distance < rhs_distance ||
+           (lhs_distance == rhs_distance && lhs_index < rhs_index);
+}
+
+// Each block independently constructs one seed's greedy candidate cluster. Threads
+// cooperate over candidates; blocks expose parallelism over all currently live seeds.
+// max_distance is updated only for the newly inserted member, which is algebraically
+// identical to rescanning all members on every iteration.
+__global__ void constructCandidateClusters(const Point* __restrict__ points,
+                                           const double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                           const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                           const int* __restrict__ seeds,
+                                           int seed_offset,
+                                           int point_count,
+                                           double threshold,
+                                           double* __restrict__ workspace,
+                                           int* __restrict__ cardinalities,
+                                           int* __restrict__ stored_members) {
+    __shared__ double reduction_distance[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int reduction_index[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int current_member;
+    __shared__ int member_count;
+
+    const int tid = threadIdx.x;
+    const int seed_position = seed_offset + static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x);
+    const int seed = seeds[seed_position];
+    double* const max_distance = workspace + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * point_count;
+
+    for (int candidate = tid; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+        max_distance[candidate] = (clustered[candidate] || candidate == seed) ? -1.0 : 0.0;
     }
-    
-    // Copy members if requested
-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;

+        member_count = 1;
+        if (stored_members) stored_members[0] = seed;
     }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (current_member >= 0) {
+        const int member = current_member;
+        double local_distance = DBL_MAX;
+        int local_index = INT_MAX;
+
+        for (int candidate = tid; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            double candidate_distance = max_distance[candidate];
+            if (candidate_distance < 0.0) continue;
+
+            double new_distance;
+            if (distances) {
+                new_distance = distances[static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count + candidate];
+            } else {
+                const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+                const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+                new_distance = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+            }
+            candidate_distance = fmax(candidate_distance, new_distance);
+            max_distance[candidate] = candidate_distance;
+            if (candidate_distance < threshold &&
+                betterCandidate(candidate_distance, candidate, local_distance, local_index)) {
+                local_distance = candidate_distance;
+                local_index = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+
+        reduction_distance[tid] = local_distance;
+        reduction_index[tid] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (tid < stride &&
+                betterCandidate(reduction_distance[tid + stride], reduction_index[tid + stride],
+                                reduction_distance[tid], reduction_index[tid])) {
+                reduction_distance[tid] = reduction_distance[tid + stride];
+                reduction_index[tid] = reduction_index[tid + stride];
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+
+        if (tid == 0) {
+            const int selected = reduction_index[0] == INT_MAX ? -1 : reduction_index[0];
+            if (selected >= 0) {
+                max_distance[selected] = -1.0;
+                if (stored_members) stored_members[member_count] = selected;
+                ++member_count;
+            }
+            current_member = selected;
+            if (selected < 0) cardinalities[seed_position] = member_count;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
 }

+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* __restrict__ points,
+                                    double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                    int point_count,
+                                    size_t pair_count) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (index >= pair_count) return;
+    const int member = static_cast<int>(index / point_count);
+    const int candidate = static_cast<int>(index - static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count);
+    const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+    const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+    distances[index] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ bool betterCandidate(double lhs_distance, int lhs_index,
+                                                double rhs_distance, int rhs_index) {
+    return lhs_distance < rhs_distance ||
+           (lhs_distance == rhs_distance && lhs_index < rhs_index);
+}
+
+// Each block independently constructs one seed's greedy candidate cluster. Threads
+// cooperate over candidates; blocks expose parallelism over all currently live seeds.
+// max_distance is updated only for the newly inserted member, which is algebraically
+// identical to rescanning all members on every iteration.
+__global__ void constructCandidateClusters(const Point* __restrict__ points,
+                                           const double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                           const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                           const int* __restrict__ seeds,
+                                           int seed_offset,
+                                           int point_count,
+                                           double threshold,
+                                           double* __restrict__ workspace,
+                                           int* __restrict__ cardinalities,
+                                           int* __restrict__ stored_members) {
+    __shared__ double reduction_distance[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int reduction_index[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int current_member;
+    __shared__ int member_count;
+
+    const int tid = threadIdx.x;
+    const int seed_position = seed_offset + static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x);
+    const int seed = seeds[seed_position];
+    double* const max_distance = workspace + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * point_count;
+
+    for (int candidate = tid; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+        max_distance[candidate] = (clustered[candidate] || candidate == seed) ? -1.0 : 0.0;
     }
-    
-    // Copy members if requested
-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;
+    if (tid == 0) {
+        current_member = seed;
+        member_count = 1;
+        if (stored_members) stored_members[0] = seed;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (current_member >= 0) {
+        const int member = current_member;
+        double local_distance = DBL_MAX;
+        int local_index = INT_MAX;
+
+        for (int candidate = tid; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            double candidate_distance = max_distance[candidate];
+            if (candidate_distance < 0.0) continue;
+
+            double new_distance;
+            if (distances) {
+                new_distance = distances[static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count + candidate];
+            } else {
+                const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+                const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+                new_distance = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+            }
+            candidate_distance = fmax(candidate_distance, new_distance);
+            max_distance[candidate] = candidate_distance;
+            if (candidate_distance < threshold &&
+                betterCandidate(candidate_distance, candidate, local_distance, local_index)) {
+                local_distance = candidate_distance;
+                local_index = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+
+        reduction_distance[tid] = local_distance;
+        reduction_index[tid] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (tid < stride &&
+                betterCandidate(reduction_distance[tid + stride], reduction_index[tid + stride],
+                                reduction_distance[tid], reduction_index[tid])) {
+                reduction_distance[tid] = reduction_distance[tid + stride];
+                reduction_index[tid] = reduction_index[tid + stride];
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+
+        if (tid == 0) {
+            const int selected = reduction_index[0] == INT_MAX ? -1 : reduction_index[0];
+            if (selected >= 0) {
+                max_distance[selected] = -1.0;
+                if (stored_members) stored_members[member_count] = selected;
+                ++member_count;
+            }
+            current_member = selected;
+            if (selected < 0) cardinalities[seed_position] = member_count;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
 }

+#define CUDA_CHECK(expr) checkCuda((expr), #expr)
+
+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* __restrict__ points,
+                                    double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                    int point_count,
+                                    size_t pair_count) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (index >= pair_count) return;
+    const int member = static_cast<int>(index / point_count);
+    const int candidate = static_cast<int>(index - static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count);
+    const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+    const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+    distances[index] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ bool betterCandidate(double lhs_distance, int lhs_index,
+                                                double rhs_distance, int rhs_index) {
+    return lhs_distance < rhs_distance ||
+           (lhs_distance == rhs_distance && lhs_index < rhs_index);
+}
+
+// Each block independently constructs one seed's greedy candidate cluster. Threads
+// cooperate over candidates; blocks expose parallelism over all currently live seeds.
+// max_distance is updated only for the newly inserted member, which is algebraically
+// identical to rescanning all members on every iteration.
+__global__ void constructCandidateClusters(const Point* __restrict__ points,
+                                           const double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                           const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                           const int* __restrict__ seeds,
+                                           int seed_offset,
+                                           int point_count,
+                                           double threshold,
+                                           double* __restrict__ workspace,
+                                           int* __restrict__ cardinalities,
+                                           int* __restrict__ stored_members) {
+    __shared__ double reduction_distance[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int reduction_index[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int current_member;
+    __shared__ int member_count;
+
+    const int tid = threadIdx.x;
+    const int seed_position = seed_offset + static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x);
+    const int seed = seeds[seed_position];
+    double* const max_distance = workspace + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * point_count;
+
+    for (int candidate = tid; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+        max_distance[candidate] = (clustered[candidate] || candidate == seed) ? -1.0 : 0.0;
+    }
+    if (tid == 0) {
+        current_member = seed;
+        member_count = 1;
+        if (stored_members) stored_members[0] = seed;
     }
-    
-    // Copy members if requested
-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (current_member >= 0) {
+        const int member = current_member;
+        double local_distance = DBL_MAX;
+        int local_index = INT_MAX;
+
+        for (int candidate = tid; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            double candidate_distance = max_distance[candidate];
+            if (candidate_distance < 0.0) continue;
+
+            double new_distance;
+            if (distances) {
+                new_distance = distances[static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count + candidate];
+            } else {
+                const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+                const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+                new_distance = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+            }
+            candidate_distance = fmax(candidate_distance, new_distance);
+            max_distance[candidate] = candidate_distance;
+            if (candidate_distance < threshold &&
+                betterCandidate(candidate_distance, candidate, local_distance, local_index)) {
+                local_distance = candidate_distance;
+                local_index = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+
+        reduction_distance[tid] = local_distance;
+        reduction_index[tid] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (tid < stride &&
+                betterCandidate(reduction_distance[tid + stride], reduction_index[tid + stride],
+                                reduction_distance[tid], reduction_index[tid])) {
+                reduction_distance[tid] = reduction_distance[tid + stride];
+                reduction_index[tid] = reduction_index[tid + stride];
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+
+        if (tid == 0) {
+            const int selected = reduction_index[0] == INT_MAX ? -1 : reduction_index[0];
+            if (selected >= 0) {
+                max_distance[selected] = -1.0;
+                if (stored_members) stored_members[member_count] = selected;
+                ++member_count;
+            }
+            current_member = selected;
+            if (selected < 0) cardinalities[seed_position] = member_count;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
 }

+#define CUDA_CHECK(expr) checkCuda((expr), #expr)

+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* __restrict__ points,
+                                    double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                    int point_count,
+                                    size_t pair_count) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (index >= pair_count) return;
+    const int member = static_cast<int>(index / point_count);
+    const int candidate = static_cast<int>(index - static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count);
+    const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+    const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+    distances[index] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ bool betterCandidate(double lhs_distance, int lhs_index,
+                                                double rhs_distance, int rhs_index) {
+    return lhs_distance < rhs_distance ||
+           (lhs_distance == rhs_distance && lhs_index < rhs_index);
+}
+
+// Each block independently constructs one seed's greedy candidate cluster. Threads
+// cooperate over candidates; blocks expose parallelism over all currently live seeds.
+// max_distance is updated only for the newly inserted member, which is algebraically
+// identical to rescanning all members on every iteration.
+__global__ void constructCandidateClusters(const Point* __restrict__ points,
+                                           const double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                           const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                           const int* __restrict__ seeds,
+                                           int seed_offset,
+                                           int point_count,
+                                           double threshold,
+                                           double* __restrict__ workspace,
+                                           int* __restrict__ cardinalities,
+                                           int* __restrict__ stored_members) {
+    __shared__ double reduction_distance[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int reduction_index[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int current_member;
+    __shared__ int member_count;
+
+    const int tid = threadIdx.x;
+    const int seed_position = seed_offset + static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x);
+    const int seed = seeds[seed_position];
+    double* const max_distance = workspace + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * point_count;
+
+    for (int candidate = tid; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+        max_distance[candidate] = (clustered[candidate] || candidate == seed) ? -1.0 : 0.0;
+    }
+    if (tid == 0) {
+        current_member = seed;
+        member_count = 1;
+        if (stored_members) stored_members[0] = seed;
     }
-    
-    // Copy members if requested

-        *cluster_members = members;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (current_member >= 0) {
+        const int member = current_member;
+        double local_distance = DBL_MAX;
+        int local_index = INT_MAX;
+
+        for (int candidate = tid; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            double candidate_distance = max_distance[candidate];
+            if (candidate_distance < 0.0) continue;
+
+            double new_distance;
+            if (distances) {
+                new_distance = distances[static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count + candidate];
+            } else {
+                const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+                const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+                new_distance = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+            }
+            candidate_distance = fmax(candidate_distance, new_distance);
+            max_distance[candidate] = candidate_distance;
+            if (candidate_distance < threshold &&
+                betterCandidate(candidate_distance, candidate, local_distance, local_index)) {
+                local_distance = candidate_distance;
+                local_index = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+
+        reduction_distance[tid] = local_distance;
+        reduction_index[tid] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (tid < stride &&
+                betterCandidate(reduction_distance[tid + stride], reduction_index[tid + stride],
+                                reduction_distance[tid], reduction_index[tid])) {
+                reduction_distance[tid] = reduction_distance[tid + stride];
+                reduction_index[tid] = reduction_index[tid + stride];
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+
+        if (tid == 0) {
+            const int selected = reduction_index[0] == INT_MAX ? -1 : reduction_index[0];
+            if (selected >= 0) {
+                max_distance[selected] = -1.0;
+                if (stored_members) stored_members[member_count] = selected;
+                ++member_count;
+            }
+            current_member = selected;
+            if (selected < 0) cardinalities[seed_position] = member_count;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
     }

-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());

I also added a memory-scalable path: it caches the full distance matrix when practical, otherwise keeps the same CUDA seed/candidate parallelism while computing distances on demand; workspace batching automatically shrinks if GPU memory is fragmented.

+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* __restrict__ points,
+                                    double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                    int point_count,
+                                    size_t pair_count) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (index >= pair_count) return;
+    const int member = static_cast<int>(index / point_count);
+    const int candidate = static_cast<int>(index - static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count);
+    const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+    const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+    distances[index] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ bool betterCandidate(double lhs_distance, int lhs_index,
+                                                double rhs_distance, int rhs_index) {
+    return lhs_distance < rhs_distance ||
+           (lhs_distance == rhs_distance && lhs_index < rhs_index);
+}
+
+// Each block independently constructs one seed's greedy candidate cluster. Threads
+// cooperate over candidates; blocks expose parallelism over all currently live seeds.
+// max_distance is updated only for the newly inserted member, which is algebraically
+// identical to rescanning all members on every iteration.
+__global__ void constructCandidateClusters(const Point* __restrict__ points,
+                                           const double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                           const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                           const int* __restrict__ seeds,
+                                           int seed_offset,
+                                           int point_count,
+                                           double threshold,
+                                           double* __restrict__ workspace,
+                                           int* __restrict__ cardinalities,
+                                           int* __restrict__ stored_members) {
+    __shared__ double reduction_distance[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int reduction_index[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int current_member;
+    __shared__ int member_count;
+
+    const int tid = threadIdx.x;
+    const int seed_position = seed_offset + static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x);
+    const int seed = seeds[seed_position];
+    double* const max_distance = workspace + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * point_count;
+
+    for (int candidate = tid; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+        max_distance[candidate] = (clustered[candidate] || candidate == seed) ? -1.0 : 0.0;
+    }
+    if (tid == 0) {
+        current_member = seed;
+        member_count = 1;
+        if (stored_members) stored_members[0] = seed;
     }
-    
-    // Copy members if requested
-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (current_member >= 0) {
+        const int member = current_member;
+        double local_distance = DBL_MAX;
+        int local_index = INT_MAX;
+
+        for (int candidate = tid; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            double candidate_distance = max_distance[candidate];
+            if (candidate_distance < 0.0) continue;
+
+            double new_distance;
+            if (distances) {
+                new_distance = distances[static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count + candidate];
+            } else {
+                const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+                const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+                new_distance = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+            }
+            candidate_distance = fmax(candidate_distance, new_distance);
+            max_distance[candidate] = candidate_distance;
+            if (candidate_distance < threshold &&
+                betterCandidate(candidate_distance, candidate, local_distance, local_index)) {
+                local_distance = candidate_distance;
+                local_index = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+
+        reduction_distance[tid] = local_distance;
+        reduction_index[tid] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (tid < stride &&
+                betterCandidate(reduction_distance[tid + stride], reduction_index[tid + stride],
+                                reduction_distance[tid], reduction_index[tid])) {
+                reduction_distance[tid] = reduction_distance[tid + stride];
+                reduction_index[tid] = reduction_index[tid + stride];
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+
+        if (tid == 0) {
+            const int selected = reduction_index[0] == INT_MAX ? -1 : reduction_index[0];
+            if (selected >= 0) {
+                max_distance[selected] = -1.0;
+                if (stored_members) stored_members[member_count] = selected;
+                ++member_count;
+            }
+            current_member = selected;
+            if (selected < 0) cardinalities[seed_position] = member_count;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());

+    const size_t distance_budget = (free_memory / 10) * 7;
+    if (matrix_size_valid && distance_bytes <= distance_budget &&

    75	// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points
    76	inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
    77	    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
    78	    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
    79	    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);

   130	    __shared__ int reduction_index[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
   131	    __shared__ int current_member;

   146	    }

   204	// Main QT clustering algorithm
   205	std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
   206	                                  const double threshold) {
   207	    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
   208	    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(N, 0);

   213	    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
   214	        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
   215	    }
   216	    
   217	    Point* device_points = nullptr;
   218	    unsigned char* device_clustered = nullptr;
   219	    int* device_seeds = nullptr;
   220	    int* device_cardinalities = nullptr;
   221	    int* device_members = nullptr;
   222	    double* device_distances = nullptr;
   223	    double* device_workspace = nullptr;
   224	
   225	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(0));
   226	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device_points),
   227	                          static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(Point)));
   228	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device_clustered),
   229	                          static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(unsigned char)));
   230	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device_seeds),
   231	                          static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(int)));
   232	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device_cardinalities),
   233	                          static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(int)));
   234	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device_members),
   235	                          static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(int)));
   236	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(device_points, points.data(), static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(Point),
   237	                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   238	
   239	    const size_t pair_count = static_cast<size_t>(N) * static_cast<size_t>(N);
   240	    if (N != 0 && pair_count / static_cast<size_t>(N) != static_cast<size_t>(N)) {
   241	        std::fprintf(stderr, "Point count is too large for CUDA indexing\n");
   242	        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
   243	    }
   244	
   245	    // Cache the all-pairs matrix whenever it fits comfortably. Large problems still
   246	    // remain fully GPU-parallel and compute distances on demand instead of failing.
   247	    size_t free_memory = 0;
   248	    size_t total_memory = 0;
   249	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemGetInfo(&free_memory, &total_memory));
   250	    (void)total_memory;
   251	    const bool matrix_size_valid =
   252	        pair_count <= std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double);
   253	    const size_t distance_bytes = matrix_size_valid
   254	                                      ? pair_count * sizeof(double)
   255	                                      : std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max();
   256	    const size_t one_row_bytes = static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(double);
   257	    const size_t distance_budget = (free_memory / 10) * 7;
   258	    if (matrix_size_valid && distance_bytes <= distance_budget &&
   259	        distance_bytes + one_row_bytes >= distance_bytes) {
   260	        const cudaError_t allocation_status =
   261	            cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device_distances), distance_bytes);
   262	        if (allocation_status == cudaSuccess) {
   263	            const size_t blocks = (pair_count + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
   264	            buildDistanceMatrix<<<static_cast<unsigned int>(blocks), CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
   265	                device_points, device_distances, N, pair_count);
   266	            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   267	        } else if (allocation_status == cudaErrorMemoryAllocation) {
   268	            // Fragmentation can make the optimistic memory query stale. The kernel's
   269	            // on-demand distance path has identical behavior and needs no matrix.
   270	            (void)cudaGetLastError();
   271	            device_distances = nullptr;
   272	        } else {
   273	            CUDA_CHECK(allocation_status);
   274	        }
   275	    }
   276	
   277	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemGetInfo(&free_memory, &total_memory));
   278	    size_t workspace_rows = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(N),
   279	                                     ((free_memory / 10) * 7) / one_row_bytes);
   280	    workspace_rows = std::max<size_t>(workspace_rows, 1);
   281	    while (true) {
   282	        const cudaError_t allocation_status = cudaMalloc(
   283	            reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device_workspace), workspace_rows * one_row_bytes);
   284	        if (allocation_status == cudaSuccess) break;
   285	        if (allocation_status != cudaErrorMemoryAllocation || workspace_rows == 1) {
   286	            CUDA_CHECK(allocation_status);
   287	        }
   288	        (void)cudaGetLastError();
   289	        workspace_rows = std::max<size_t>(workspace_rows / 2, 1);
   290	    }
   291	
   292	    std::vector<int> cardinalities(N);
   293	
   294	    // Main clustering loop
   295	    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
   296	        int max_cardinality = -1;
   297	        size_t best_seed_position = 0;
   298	
   299	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(device_clustered, clustered.data(),
   300	                              static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(unsigned char), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   301	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(device_seeds, unclustered_indices.data(),
   302	                              unclustered_indices.size() * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   303	
   304	        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < unclustered_indices.size(); offset += workspace_rows) {
   305	            const size_t batch_size = std::min(workspace_rows, unclustered_indices.size() - offset);
   306	            constructCandidateClusters<<<static_cast<unsigned int>(batch_size), CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
   307	                device_points, device_distances, device_clustered, device_seeds,
   308	                static_cast<int>(offset), N, threshold, device_workspace,
   309	                device_cardinalities, nullptr);
   310	            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   311	        }
   312	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(cardinalities.data(), device_cardinalities,
   313	                              unclustered_indices.size() * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
   314	
   315	        // A strict comparison in ascending seed order reproduces the CPU tie-break.
   316	        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {
   317	            if (cardinalities[i] > max_cardinality) {
   318	                max_cardinality = cardinalities[i];
   319	                best_seed_position = i;
   320	            }
   321	        }
   322	
   323	        // If every candidate is a singleton, no pair of remaining points is within
   324	        // the threshold. Subsequent iterations can only emit the same ascending
   325	        // sequence of singleton clusters, so finish it without redundant launches.
   326	        if (max_cardinality == 1) {
   327	            for (const int seed : unclustered_indices) {
   328	                Cluster cluster;
   329	                cluster.seed_point = seed;
   330	                cluster.members.push_back(seed);
   331	                clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
   332	            }
   333	            break;
   334	        }
   335	
   336	        constructCandidateClusters<<<1, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
   337	            device_points, device_distances, device_clustered, device_seeds,
   338	            static_cast<int>(best_seed_position), N, threshold, device_workspace,
   339	            device_cardinalities, device_members);
   340	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   341	
   342	        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members(static_cast<size_t>(max_cardinality));
   343	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(best_cluster_members.data(), device_members,
   344	                              best_cluster_members.size() * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
   345	        const int best_seed = unclustered_indices[best_seed_position];
   346	        
   347	        // If we found a cluster, add it
   348	        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
   349	            Cluster cluster;
   350	            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
   351	            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
   352	            clusters.push_back(cluster);
   353	            
   354	            // Mark all members as clustered
   355	            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
   356	                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = 1;
   357	            }
   358	            
   359	            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
   360	            unclustered_indices.erase(

+#define CUDA_CHECK(expr) checkCuda((expr), #expr)
+
+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* __restrict__ points,
+                                    double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                    int point_count,
+                                    size_t pair_count) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (index >= pair_count) return;
+    const int member = static_cast<int>(index / point_count);
+    const int candidate = static_cast<int>(index - static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count);
+    const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+    const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+    distances[index] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ bool betterCandidate(double lhs_distance, int lhs_index,
+                                                double rhs_distance, int rhs_index) {
+    return lhs_distance < rhs_distance ||
+           (lhs_distance == rhs_distance && lhs_index < rhs_index);
+}
+
+// Each block independently constructs one seed's greedy candidate cluster. Threads
+// cooperate over candidates; blocks expose parallelism over all currently live seeds.
+// max_distance is updated only for the newly inserted member, which is algebraically
+// identical to rescanning all members on every iteration.
+__global__ void constructCandidateClusters(const Point* __restrict__ points,
+                                           const double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                           const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                           const int* __restrict__ seeds,
+                                           int seed_offset,
+                                           int point_count,
+                                           double threshold,
+                                           double* __restrict__ workspace,
+                                           int* __restrict__ cardinalities,
+                                           int* __restrict__ stored_members) {
+    __shared__ double reduction_distance[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int reduction_index[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int current_member;
+    __shared__ int member_count;
+
+    const int tid = threadIdx.x;
+    const int seed_position = seed_offset + static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x);
+    const int seed = seeds[seed_position];
+    double* const max_distance = workspace + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * point_count;
+
+    for (int candidate = tid; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+        max_distance[candidate] = (clustered[candidate] || candidate == seed) ? -1.0 : 0.0;
+    }
+    if (tid == 0) {
+        current_member = seed;
+        member_count = 1;
+        if (stored_members) stored_members[0] = seed;
     }
-    
-    // Copy members if requested
-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (current_member >= 0) {
+        const int member = current_member;
+        double local_distance = DBL_MAX;
+        int local_index = INT_MAX;
+
+        for (int candidate = tid; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            double candidate_distance = max_distance[candidate];
+            if (candidate_distance < 0.0) continue;
+
+            double new_distance;
+            if (distances) {
+                new_distance = distances[static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count + candidate];
+            } else {
+                const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+                const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+                new_distance = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+            }
+            candidate_distance = fmax(candidate_distance, new_distance);
+            max_distance[candidate] = candidate_distance;
+            if (candidate_distance < threshold &&
+                betterCandidate(candidate_distance, candidate, local_distance, local_index)) {
+                local_distance = candidate_distance;
+                local_index = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+
+        reduction_distance[tid] = local_distance;
+        reduction_index[tid] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (tid < stride &&
+                betterCandidate(reduction_distance[tid + stride], reduction_index[tid + stride],
+                                reduction_distance[tid], reduction_index[tid])) {
+                reduction_distance[tid] = reduction_distance[tid + stride];
+                reduction_index[tid] = reduction_index[tid + stride];
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+
+        if (tid == 0) {
+            const int selected = reduction_index[0] == INT_MAX ? -1 : reduction_index[0];
+            if (selected >= 0) {
+                max_distance[selected] = -1.0;
+                if (stored_members) stored_members[member_count] = selected;
+                ++member_count;
+            }
+            current_member = selected;
+            if (selected < 0) cardinalities[seed_position] = member_count;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
 }

+#define CUDA_CHECK(expr) checkCuda((expr), #expr)
